<div class="shopify-section" id="shopify-section-pm-features">
<h1 class="pm_heading_primary">Designed by you, <span>delivered by us</span></h1>
<div class="pm_homepage_features_row">
<div class="pm_homepage_features_col">
<img src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0078/8214/5850/files/Ico_Minimums_9e75a04b-70c0-4364-9119-d5b7275c2888_128x_compressed_128x.png?v=1554834078"/>
<h3>No Minimums</h3>
<p>With zero minimums, it's never been easier to get started.</p>

<script>
  var access_token = "1945697959.1677ed0.545b6fffb97a47ccab5a029acec66852"
  var limit = 5
  $.ajax("https://api.instagram.com/v1/users/self/media/recent", {
    method: "GET",
    data: {
      access_token: access_token,
      count: limit
    }
  }).done(function(res) {
    var containers = $('.dynamic-instagram-pm-container');

    res.data.forEach(function(d, index) {
      var $container = $(containers[index]);
      renderImage($container, d)
    })
  })

  function renderImage($container, image) {
    $container.find('.dynamic-instagram-pm-image').attr('src', image.images.low_resolution.url)
    $container.find('.dynamic-instagram-pm-link').attr('href', image.link)
  }
</script>
